Abstract: We investigate how nonmetricity affects particle creation and evaporation in rotating bumblebee black holes by comparing metric and metric-affine solutions. We normalize the stationary Killing vectors at spatial infinity before defining physical frequencies, angular velocities, and temperatures. This procedure changes the static comparison and gives the leading calibration $\ell=3X/4$. In the metric solution, $\ell$ shifts the horizons, stationary limit surfaces, and extremal boundary, increases the normalized horizon angular velocity, and suppresses the Hawking temperature. Nevertheless, the surface gravity remains uniform and the scalar wave equation separable. We derive the tunneling factors, quantum occupation numbers, radial potential, and an analytical lower bound for the axisymmetric greybody factor. In the metric-affine geometry, nonmetricity preserves the Kerr coordinate locations of the horizons and stationary limit surfaces, but changes the physical horizon area, normalized angular velocity, and meridional sector. The component $g_{rθ}$ couples angular channels, whereas the local surface gravity depends on latitude when $aX\neq0$. Consequently, the generic rotating configuration admits neither a single global Hawking temperature nor an independent channel-by-channel emission spectrum. In the static limit, under the same Stefan-Boltzmann prescription, both Lorentz-violating geometries have lower luminosities and longer lifetimes, with the metric-affine black hole radiating less and evaporating more slowly. A local slow-rotation expansion preserves this tendency but does not establish a global rotating evaporation hierarchy. Existing weak-field constraints limit fractional corrections to static quantum-emission observables to below $1.3\times10^{-11}$.

</details>

Result: 成功推导出避免对数奇点的初始数据显式条件和充要积分条件；构造了简单情况下的精确初始数据示例；开发了计算一般情况完整初始数据集的数值程序；数值演化结果展示了系统从$\mathscr{I}^-$到$\mathscr{I}^+$的稳定传播，验证了两种紧化规范下方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究为引力辐射的全局数值模拟框架奠定了基础，明确了过去类光无穷$\mathscr{I}^-$上初始数据在决定渐近结构中的关键作用。通过推导正则性条件和实现稳定的数值演化，证明了可以在共形紧化时空中实现从$\mathscr{I}^-$到$\mathscr{I}^+$的完整演化过程，为理解引力波的全局传播行为提供了重要的理论和计算工具。

Abstract: We study the linearised spin-2 field on conformally compactified Minkowski spacetime. Initial data are prescribed on past null infinity $\mathscr{I}^-$, and the system is evolved through the cylinder at spatial infinity to future null infinity $\mathscr{I}^+$.
  To avoid the development of logarithmic singularities and thereby preserve smooth peeling behaviour, we derive explicit conditions on the freely specifiable ingoing gravitational radiation $ψ_0$ at $\mathscr{I}^-$ that ensure regular initial data. For compactly supported data, we obtain necessary and sufficient integral conditions guaranteeing regularity and compact support of the full system. In the simplest cases, we construct examples of exact initial data, while for the general case, we present a numerical procedure that computes the remaining $ψ_k$ to obtain a full initial data set.
  We perform numerical evolutions using two gauges corresponding to compactification of either a neighbourhood of spatial infinity or the entire Minkowski spacetime, and demonstrate stable propagation of the system from $\mathscr{I}^-$ to $\mathscr{I}^+$.
  These results provide a step towards a framework for fully global simulations of gravitational radiation and clarify the role of initial data on $\mathscr{I}^-$ in determining asymptotic structure.

</details>

Result: 研究发现:对于二次势,较小质量值更容易获得足够的e-folds数;而对于Starobinsky势,较大质量值产生更多e-folds。慢滚暴胀概率在所有情况下均非常接近1。三种方法计算的功率谱呈现相同定性模式:小k处被抑制,中间范围放大并振荡,大k处近似标度不变。三种方法的主要区别在于收敛到标度不变区域的速度,混合方法最快,其次是替代质量函数方法和修饰度规方法。枢轴尺度k★对暴胀子质量高度敏感,质量仅变化几个百分点,k★就会改变超过一个数量级。计算得到的标量谱指数ns和张量标量比r与当前观测数据符合良好。在高多极矩处,三种方法均与Planck 2018数据一致;在低多极矩处,混合方法符合最好,修饰度规方法偏差最大。

Conclusion: 在环量子宇宙学框架下,将质量参数作为自由现象学参数处理的标量场暴胀模型能够成功产生足够的暴胀e-folds数和与观测一致的原初功率谱。三种不同的功率谱计算方法(修饰度规、混合和替代质量函数方法)在定性上给出一致的结果,均能在高多极矩处与Planck 2018观测数据符合,但在低多极矩处存在差异,其中混合方法表现最优。枢轴尺度对暴胀子质量的高敏感性为未来精确测量提供了重要的观测窗口。研究结果支持了环量子宇宙学框架下暴胀模型的可行性,并为区分不同量子引力效应的计算方案提供了观测依据。

Abstract: Considering the mass parameters as free phenomenological parameters, we investigate inflation driven by a canonical scalar field with different potentials in LQC. We find that a smaller mass value makes it easier to obtain a sufficient number of e-folds for the quadratic potential, while for the Starobinsky potential, more e-folds are obtained for larger mass values. By estimating the probability of slow-roll inflation, we find that it remains very close to unity for all considered cases. We compute the primordial power spectrum using three approaches: the dressed metric, the hybrid, and the alternative mass function approaches for the case that the kinetic energy dominated at bounce. The resulting power spectrum for both potentials and different mass values shows the same qualitative pattern in all three approaches. It is suppressed at small $k$, amplified and oscillating over an intermediate range, and nearly scale-invariant at large $k$. The approaches mainly differ in how fast the power spectrum converges to this regime, with the hybrid approach converging the fastest, followed by the alternative mass function and dressed metric approaches. We find that the pivot scale $k_\star$ is highly sensitive to the inflaton mass, changing by more than an order of magnitude for a mass variation of only a few percent. Using the tensor power spectrum at $k_\star$, we calculate the scalar spectral index $n_s$ and tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$, finding good agreement with current data. Finally, the scalar power spectra are fed into the CAMB code to obtain the angular power spectrum and compare with the Planck 2018 data and the best-fit $Λ$CDM model. All three approaches are consistent with data at high multipoles, while at low multipoles the hybrid approach gives the closest agreement and the dressed metric approach shows the largest deviation.

</details>


### [133] [Cosmological dynamics based on Lovelock's gravity. Qualitative analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01702)
*Aleksei V. Nikolaev,Victor M. Zhuravlev,Sergei V. Chervon*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对Lovelock引力理论在平坦FRW宇宙中的宇宙学动力学进行了完整的定性分析,将动力学简化为关于Hubble参数的单一自治一阶方程,系统地分类了可能的演化情景,并发现了一种称为"大激波"(Big Shock)的新型宇宙起源机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准广义相对论在描述宇宙早期演化时存在局限性,而Lovelock引力作为爱因斯坦引力在高维时空中的自然推广,可能提供新的宇宙学图景。研究者希望系统地理解Lovelock引力理论框架下宇宙演化的所有可能情景,特别是探索在高维空间和特定耦合常数条件下是否存在不同于标准大爆炸的宇宙起源机制。

Method: 从N维广义Friedmann方程出发,利用Riemann张量的独立分量将宇宙学动力学简化为关于Hubble参数H的单一自治一阶微分方程。该方程的右侧是H²的多项式比值,由Lovelock多项式的阶数n、维数N和耦合常数α_i确定。通过分析这些多项式的实根来确定系统的不动点和奇异点,进而研究系统的渐近行为。对不动点的吸引/排斥性质进行分类,识别有效物质密度为负的"幽灵区间",并分析到达不动点和奇异点所需的时间特性。

Result: 完整分类了所有可能的宇宙演化情景,确定了吸引和排斥不动点的位置和性质,定位了"幽灵区间"的分布。证明了不动点在无限时间内到达而奇异点在有限时间内到达。发现了一种定性上全新的情景——"大激波":当N>4且耦合常数取适当负值时,宇宙从有限密度和有限尺度因子但Hubble参数变化率无穷大的状态开始演化,这取代了标准大爆炸图景。通过n=1,N=4(广义相对论)、n=2,N=5(Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力)和n=3,N=7(三次Lovelock引力)的具体算例验证了一般分析框架。

Conclusion: Lovelock引力理论在平坦FRW宇宙中展现出丰富的宇宙学动力学行为,其演化完全由系统不动点和奇异点的分布决定。特别重要的是,在高维空间(N>4)和适当的负耦合常数条件下,理论预言了"大激波"这一全新的宇宙起源机制,它在初始时刻具有有限的物质密度和尺度因子但无穷大的Hubble参数变化率,从而为替代标准大爆炸模型提供了理论可能性。该研究为理解高维引力理论的宇宙学效应提供了系统的数学框架和完整的物理图景。

Abstract: We perform a complete qualitative analysis of the cosmological dynamics of Lovelock gravity in a spatially flat Friedmann-Robertson-Walker (FRW) universe filled with a perfect fluid obeying a barotropic equation of state $p=ωρ$. Starting from the generalized $N$-dimensional Friedmann equations written in terms of the independent components of the Riemann tensor, we reduce the dynamics to a single autonomous first-order equation for the Hubble parameter $H$, whose right-hand side is a ratio of polynomials in $H^2$ fixed by the order $n$ of the Lovelock polynomial, the number of dimensions $N$, and the coupling constants $α_i$. The real roots of these polynomials determine the fixed points and the singular points of the system, which govern its asymptotic behaviour. We give a complete classification of the possible evolution scenarios, identify the attractive and repulsive fixed points, locate the "phantom intervals" in which the effective matter density becomes negative, and show that fixed points are reached in infinite time whereas singular points are reached in finite time. For $N>4$ and suitable negative couplings a qualitatively new scenario arises, which we call the "Big Shock": the universe starts from a state with finite density and finite scale factor but with an infinite rate of change of the Hubble parameter, replacing the standard Big Bang. The general analysis is illustrated for $n=1$, $N=4$ (general relativity), $n=2$, $N=5$ (Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity), and $n=3$, $N=7$ (cubic Lovelock gravity).

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了偏心非自旋致密双星系统保守动力学中尾-尾效应(tail-of-tail)对相对1PN阶和$\mathcal{O}(e_t^{12})$的贡献,通过准开普勒动力学推导了5.5PN和6.5PN阶的Delaunay平均哈密顿量,并将其与有效单体(EOB)描述匹配,确定了非测地线EOB Q势的贡献,同时验证了一阶自力结果并提供了新的二阶自力预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波天文学对高精度波形模型的需求推动了致密双星系统动力学的精确计算。尾-尾效应是后牛顿展开中的重要高阶项,对偏心轨道系统的保守动力学有显著贡献。现有研究缺乏对偏心轨道情况下尾-尾项的完整处理,特别是在二阶自力(second-order self-force)层面。本研究旨在填补这一理论空白,为引力波数据分析提供更精确的理论预测。

Method: 1. 使用调和坐标系下的1PN准开普勒动力学框架;2. 推导5.5PN和6.5PN阶的Delaunay平均哈密顿量,精确到$\mathcal{O}(e_t^{12})$;3. 将结果与有效单体(EOB)理论描述进行匹配,确定非测地线EOB Q势的贡献至$\mathcal{O}(p_r^{12})$,包括对称质量比$\nu$至$\mathcal{O}(\nu^2)$的依赖关系;4. 使用Fourier-Bessel分解方法独立重新推导遗传相互作用的平均哈密顿量;5. 在固定轨道频率下应用第一定律,恢复和验证红移相关结果。

Result: 1. 成功计算了尾-尾效应对偏心致密双星保守动力学的相对1PN阶贡献;2. 获得了5.5PN和6.5PN阶的完整Delaunay平均哈密顿量至$\mathcal{O}(e_t^{12})$;3. 确定了非测地线EOB Q势至$\mathcal{O}(p_r^{12})$的贡献,包括对称质量比的二阶依赖;4. 质量比的线性项与已有一阶自力结果完全一致,验证了方法的正确性;5. 二次项提供了源自尾-尾项的全新偏心二阶自力定性预测;6. 恢复了已知的一阶自力红移至$\mathcal{O}(e^{12})$;7. 获得了5.5PN和6.5PN阶尾-尾效应对二阶逆红移的完整贡献至$\mathcal{O}(e^{10})$。

Conclusion: 本研究系统计算了偏心致密双星系统中尾-尾效应对保守动力学的高阶后牛顿贡献,成功推导了5.5PN和6.5PN阶的平均哈密顿量并与EOB理论框架匹配。结果不仅验证了现有一阶自力计算的正确性,更重要的是提供了偏心轨道情况下二阶自力效应的全新理论预测。通过两种独立方法(准开普勒动力学和Fourier-Bessel分解)的交叉验证增强了结果的可靠性。这些高精度理论预测对引力波数据分析、双星系统参数估计以及检验广义相对论具有重要价值,为未来引力波探测器观测偏心轨道合并事件提供了必要的理论基础。

Abstract: We compute the tail-of-tail contribution to the conservative dynamics of eccentric, non-spinning compact binaries to relative 1PN order and to $\mathcal O(e_t^{12})$. Using the $1$PN quasi-Keplerian dynamics in harmonic coordinates, we derive the Delaunay-averaged Hamiltonian at $5.5$PN and $6.5$PN order and match it to the effective-one-body description, allowing us to determine the corresponding contributions to the non-geodesic EOB $Q$ potential through the $\mathcal{O}(p_r^{12})$, including the dependence on the symmetric mass ratio up to $\mathcal{O}(ν^2)$. The terms linear in the mass ratio reproduce the available first-order self-force results, while the quadratic terms provide qualitatively new eccentric second-order self-force predictions arising from the tail-of-tail terms. We independently rederive the averaged Hamiltonian using a Fourier--Bessel decomposition of the hereditary interaction. Applying the first law at fixed orbital frequencies, we recover the known first-order self-force redshift through $\mathcal O(e^{12})$ and obtain the complete tail-of-tail contribution to the second-order inverse redshift at $5.5$PN and $6.5$PN through $\mathcal O(e^{10})$.

</details>


### [135] [A Quantum Dominant Energy Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01814)
*Stefan Hollands,Roberto Longo,Gerardo Morsella*

Abstract: We propose a quantum dominant energy condition (QDEC) for the stress tensor in the context of quantum field theory in curved spacetimes. A rigorous proof is given for the case of Rindler wedges, and a heuristic discussion about possible generalizations to more general geometric setups, including curved spacetime, is provided. In Minkowski spacetime, we establish a connection with state recovery bounds. We illustrate the QDEC for coherent states of the free scalar field, where it turns out to be related to the ordinary DEC for the stress tensor of the classical solution that defines the coherent state.

</details>

Abstract: In the framework of the Iyer-Wald formalism, the first law of black hole mechanics is examined within the context of conformal Einstein-power-Yang-Mills (CEPYM) theory. Abstract: Higher-curvature gravity usually leads to very complicated field equations, which makes it hard to find analytical solutions. In this work, we obtain analytical charged black hole solutions in higher-curvature gravity by using black hole thermodynamics and the continued fraction expansion. We study the thermodynamics of static black holes using the first law of thermodynamics and the Smarr formula. We show that our results agree with those obtained by directly solving the field equations in Einstein gravity.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 成功建立了FLRW背景上标量场的Bateman和CK形式体系之间的显式正则变换。因子a^3(t)和a^(-3)(t)分别生成阻尼和反阻尼CK扇区。证明了含有Ḣ(t)项对于哈密顿量等价性是必需的。在旋转变量表示下,Bateman标量场哈密顿量在常数H时守恒。特别地,对于幂律背景a(t)∝t^p,发现在p=2/3时存在相关族,尽管H(t)时间依赖,但H_B,SF仍然守恒。

Conclusion: 本文成功将耗散方程的Bateman和CK变分形式体系扩展到FLRW宇宙学背景上的均匀标量场,建立了两者之间完整的正则对应关系。研究表明Ḣ(t)项对哈密顿量等价性至关重要,并发现在特定幂律膨胀参数p=2/3时存在特殊的守恒结构。这些结果仅限于预设FLRW背景上的经典均匀场,不包含引力相空间。该工作为宇宙学背景下耗散系统的变分描述提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: Dissipative equations admit distinct variational descriptions in the Bateman and Caldirola--Kanai (CK) formalisms. The classical correspondence between them is extended to a homogeneous canonical scalar field on a prescribed spatially flat Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker (FLRW) background, where the expansion produces the time-dependent damping coefficient $3H(t)$. A multiplier action yields the Klein--Gordon equation and a complementary anti-damped equation containing the term $-3\dot{H}(t)χ$. A first-order Bateman Lagrangian derived from the same multiplier action reproduces this physical--auxiliary pair for a general potential. Specializing to a free massive field gives the Bateman and doubled CK Lagrangians and Hamiltonians used in the canonical comparison. The factors $a^{3}(t)$ and $a^{-3}(t)$ generate the damped and anti-damped CK sectors, respectively. An explicit time-dependent canonical transformation, generated by a function linear in the Bateman momenta, maps the complete doubled CK system to the Bateman system. For this point transformation, the terms proportional to $\dot{H}(t)$ are required for Hamiltonian equivalence. In rotated variables, the Bateman scalar-field Hamiltonian takes the difference form $H_{B,\mathrm{SF}} = E_{u} - E_{v}$. It is conserved for constant $H$ and generally varies with time otherwise. For the power-law background $a(t) \propto t^{p}$, however, a correlated family at $p = 2/3$ has conserved $H_{B,\mathrm{SF}}$ despite the time dependence of $H(t)$. These results concern classical homogeneous fields on a prescribed FLRW background and exclude the gravitational phase space.

</details>


### [139] [Superradiant Bose--Einstein condensates around Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02051)
*Sen Guo,Lin Wen,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究要么在静态背景下保留自相互作用，要么在Kerr度规下忽略自相互作用，缺乏对旋转黑洞周围玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚态暗物质云的完整理论描述。需要同时处理旋转黑洞的时空几何效应和暗物质场的非线性自相互作用，以准确预测超辐射云的物理性质和结构特征。

Method: 从带四次势的Klein-Gordon方程出发，将线性问题分解为球旋波和径向方程并自洽求解；利用守恒的Noether流计算超辐射增长率；将非线性项投影到单模上，得到Gross-Pitaevskii方程；采用边界牛顿法在固定粒子数条件下对方程进行数值积分求解。通过场的重新标度，使整个解族依赖于单一无量纲参数𝒩=λN。

Result: 恢复了引力原子的类氢光谱和增长率的α^(4ℓ+5)标度律；获得了精确关系Jz=ℏmN，该关系不受自相互作用修正；发现凝聚态呈环形结构而非球壳，密度在旋转轴上恒为零；旋转通过几何因子Δ(r)调制自相互作用，使其在视界处关闭；云团性质仅在𝒩≳10³时才发生显著改变。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了旋转黑洞周围自相互作用玻色子暗物质云的完整理论框架，揭示了旋转对自相互作用的几何调制效应，证明了角动量守恒关系不受非线性项影响，并预测了凝聚态的环形拓扑结构。结果表明只有当无量纲耦合参数足够大(𝒩≳10³)时，自相互作用才会显著改变超辐射云的宏观性质，为探测超轻暗物质提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Ultralight bosonic dark matter can accumulate around a rotating black hole, where superradiance amplifies the field until a macroscopic cloud forms. Whether such a cloud behaves as a Bose--Einstein condensate depends on the self-interaction, which earlier work has either retained on static backgrounds or dropped on the Kerr metric. Here we treat the two together. Starting from the Klein--Gordon equation with a quartic potential, we separate the linear problem into spheroidal and radial equations and solve them self-consistently, obtain the superradiant growth rate from the conserved Noether current, project the nonlinear term onto a single mode, and integrate the resulting Gross--Pitaevskii equation with a bordered Newton method at fixed particle number. Rotation modulates the self-interaction geometrically: the effective coupling carries a factor $Δ(r)$ and therefore switches off at the horizon. Once the field is rescaled, the whole solution family depends on the single dimensionless parameter $\mathcal{N}=λN$. We recover the hydrogenic spectrum of the gravitational atom and the $α^{4\ell+5}$ scaling of the growth rate, and we obtain the exact relation $J_{z}=\hbar mN$, which receives no correction from the self-interaction. We find that the condensate is a torus rather than a spherical shell, with its density vanishing identically on the rotation axis, and that the cloud is modified appreciably only for $\mathcal{N}\gtrsim10^{3}$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究仅保留单个非最小曲率耦合项，未能全面刻画洛伦兹对称性破缺引力理论中带电黑洞的完整物理图像。需要同时考虑两个独立的非最小曲率耦合的联合效应，并系统分析其热力学性质和拓扑特征，以深入理解洛伦兹破缺背景下黑洞的物理本质。

Method: 1) 在含背景Kalb-Ramond场的洛伦兹对称性破缺引力理论框架下，同时引入两个独立的非最小曲率耦合项；2) 求解球对称带电黑洞的精确静态解，包括有/无宇宙学常数的情形；3) 采用Iyer-Wald协变相空间形式主义推导修正的热力学量；4) 分析Joule-Thomson膨胀过程，确定反转曲线和冷却/加热区域；5) 应用黑洞热力学的拓扑方法表征类van der Waals相变，通过拓扑缺陷曲线编码临界温度和压强。

Result: 获得了同时包含两个非最小曲率耦合的带电黑洞精确解（含/不含宇宙学常数）；推导出修正的热力学量，包括质量、熵、温度等；确定了Joule-Thomson膨胀的反转曲线以及冷却和加热区域的分布；通过拓扑分析揭示了类van der Waals相变结构，热力学临界点信息被编码在拓扑缺陷曲线中；明确了洛伦兹破缺参数对黑洞热力学和拓扑性质的影响规律。

Conclusion: 本研究系统阐明了洛伦兹对称性破缺引力理论中带电黑洞的热力学和拓扑特征。两个非最小曲率耦合的联合效应对黑洞解及其热力学性质产生显著影响。Joule-Thomson分析和拓扑方法为理解这类黑洞的相变行为提供了互补视角，揭示了洛伦兹破缺背景场对黑洞物理的本质作用，为进一步研究量子引力和洛伦兹对称性破缺现象学提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate exact static, spherically symmetric electrically charged black hole solutions in a gravitational theory with spontaneous Lorentz-symmetry breaking induced by a background Kalb--Ramond field. In contrast to previous analyses that retained only one nonminimal curvature coupling, we include the combined effects of the two independent nonminimal curvature couplings and obtain charged black hole solutions both with and without a cosmological constant. Using the Iyer--Wald covariant phase-space formalism, we derive the corrected thermodynamic quantities and analyze the Joule--Thomson expansion, including the inversion curve and the cooling/heating regions. We further apply the topological approach to black hole thermodynamics to characterize the van der Waals-like phase transition and show how the thermodynamic critical temperature and pressure are encoded in the corresponding topological defect curve. These results clarify the thermodynamic and topological signatures of electrically charged black holes in gravity with a Lorentz-violating Kalb--Ramond background.

</details>


### [141] [Hidden Symmetry of Kerr-deSitter from Manifest Symmetry of Painlevé VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02259)
*Stefan Hollands,Akihiro Ishibashi,Jochen Zahn*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过Painlevé VI方程的等单值变形理论,解释了Kerr-deSitter黑洞径向Teukolsky方程的"质量对称性",并发现对偶时空也代表黑洞,从而用几何方法重新得到了已知的模稳定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用经典的等单值变形理论,建立Teukolsky方程的质量对称性与Painlevé VI方程对称性之间的联系。通过分析质量对称变换后的波动方程,研究对偶时空的几何结构,并识别其黑洞特征。运用这种几何洞察来重新推导模稳定性结果。

Result: 成功证明了径向Teukolsky方程的质量对称性可以从Painlevé VI方程的对称性通过等单值变形理论导出。发现对偶时空本身也表示一个黑洞结构。以几何方式重新获得了先前已知的部分模稳定性结果,并揭示了对偶时空的其他特殊几何特征。

Conclusion: 本研究从数学物理的深层联系出发,通过Painlevé VI方程与等单值变形理论阐明了Kerr-deSitter黑洞Teukolsky方程质量对称性的本质。对偶时空的黑洞性质为理解模稳定性提供了新的几何视角,这种联系深化了对黑洞扰动理论与可积系统之间关系的认识。

Abstract: We show that the recently discovered ``mass symmetries" of the radial Teukolsky equation for Kerr-deSitter black holes can be understood from a corresponding symmetry of the Painlevé VI equation via the classical theory of ``isomonodromic deformations". It is known that a subset of the mass symmetries transforms the Teukolsky equation to a wave equation on a ``dual" Lorentzian spacetime. We find that this dual spacetime again represents a black hole, and use this insight to re-obtain a previously known partial mode stability result in a geometric manner. Further special geometric features of the dual spacetime are pointed out.

</details>


### [142] [A parity selection rule for regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02481)
*Farid Thaalba,Julio Arrechea,Stefano Liberati*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了球对称引力场方程中规则黑洞理论必须满足的局部一致性条件，发现曲率正则性不足以保证动力学一致性，并建立了理论函数的奇偶性选择规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则黑洞度规通常仅从运动学角度研究，但有限曲率的静态核心并不能保证底层理论能够一致地演化通过规则中心的一般物质。需要建立动力学一致性的必要条件来筛选真正可行的规则黑洞理论。

Method: 在球对称条件下，对最一般的基于作用量、恒等守恒的二阶引力场方程类推导局部一致性条件。通过分析定义理论的两个函数在有符号径向坐标反演下的性质，结合中心正则性和非退化条件，建立选择规则。对可积分部分，使用广义Misner-Sharp-Hernandez质量的奇偶性进行等价表述。对从静态单参数真空族重构的理论，条件转化为半径与质量同时反演下的协变性。

Result: Hayward几何和Dymnikova几何对应的理论满足该选择规则，而Bardeen理论不满足，表明静态解的曲率正则性不足以保证与一般物质的动力学一致性。刻画了包含具有德西特核心的Hayward型黑洞的无穷类可容许理论。选择规则要求广义质量在中心处为奇函数、三次方消失且不含点质量贡献。

Conclusion: 该选择规则为描述规则塌缩的理论提供了必要条件，但其本身不能确立适定性或保证非奇异终态。静态解的曲率正则性是不充分的，动力学一致性需要理论函数满足特定的对称性和正则性要求。这为构造和筛选物理上可行的规则黑洞理论提供了重要的理论判据。

Abstract: Regular black hole metrics are usually studied kinematically, but a finite-curvature static core does not guarantee that the underlying theory can consistently evolve generic matter through a regular center. We derive a necessary local consistency condition within the most general class of action-based, identically conserved, second-order gravitational field equations in spherical symmetry. Regularity requires that the two functions defining the theory have opposite parities under reversal of the signed radial coordinate, together with additional center-regularity and nondegeneracy conditions. In the integrable sector, this criterion is equivalent to requiring the generalized Misner--Sharp--Hernandez mass to be odd across the center, to vanish cubically there, and to contain no point-mass contribution. For theories reconstructed from static one-parameter vacuum families, the condition becomes covariance under simultaneous reversal of radius and mass. The theories associated with the Hayward and Dymnikova geometries satisfy this selection rule. In contrast, the Bardeen theory does not, demonstrating that curvature regularity of a static solution is insufficient for dynamical consistency with generic matter. We also characterize an infinite class of admissible theories containing Hayward-like black holes with de Sitter cores. The selection rule provides a necessary condition for theories intended to describe regular collapse, but does not by itself establish well-posedness or guarantee a nonsingular endpoint.

</details>
